    1703             : /* -------------------------------------------------------------------- */
    1704             : /** \name Pie Menu Direction
    1705             :  *
    1706             :  * This goes in a seemingly weird pattern:
    1707             :  *
    1708             :  * <pre>
    1709             :  *     4
    1710             :  *  5     6
    1711             :  * 1       2
    1712             :  *  7     8
    1713             :  *     3
    1714             :  * </pre>
    1715             :  *
    1716             :  * but it's actually quite logical. It's designed to be 'upwards compatible'
    1717             :  * for muscle memory so that the menu item locations are fixed and don't move
    1718             :  * as new items are added to the menu later on. It also optimizes efficiency -
    1719             :  * a radial menu is best kept symmetrical, with as large an angle between
    1720             :  * items as possible, so that the gestural mouse movements can be fast and inexact.
    1721             :  *
    1722             :  * It starts off with two opposite sides for the first two items
    1723             :  * then joined by the one below for the third (this way, even with three items,
    1724             :  * the menu seems to still be 'in order' reading left to right). Then the fourth is
    1725             :  * added to complete the compass directions. From here, it's just a matter of
    1726             :  * subdividing the rest of the angles for the last 4 items.
    1727             :  *
    1728             :  * --Matt 07/2006
    1729             :  * \{ */
    1730             : 
    1731             : const char radial_dir_order[8] = {
    1732             :     UI_RADIAL_W,
    1733             :     UI_RADIAL_E,
    1734             :     UI_RADIAL_S,
    1735             :     UI_RADIAL_N,
    1736             :     UI_RADIAL_NW,
    1737             :     UI_RADIAL_NE,
    1738             :     UI_RADIAL_SW,
    1739             :     UI_RADIAL_SE,
    1740             : };
    1741             : 
    1742             : const char radial_dir_to_numpad[8] = {8, 9, 6, 3, 2, 1, 4, 7};
    1743             : const short radial_dir_to_angle[8] = {90, 45, 0, 315, 270, 225, 180, 135};
    1744             : 
    1745           0 : static std::string but_pie_direction_string(const Button *but)
    1746             : {
    1747           0 :   BLI_assert(but->pie_dir < ARRAY_SIZE(radial_dir_to_numpad));
    1748           0 :   return fmt::to_string(int(radial_dir_to_numpad[but->pie_dir]));
    1749             : }
    1750             : 
    1751             : /** \} */
